# Hook swipe file: verified LinkedIn hooks from data, AI and LinkedIn-growth creators

Every hook below comes from a public LinkedIn post that was fetched and checked on Oct 14, 2026. Each post has **100 or more reactions**. Reaction counts are exact as of the fetch. Impressions are private on LinkedIn. Personal-profile engagement runs about 2-3% of impressions ([AuthoredUp](https://authoredup.com/blog/linkedin-metrics-to-track)), mostly reactions, so 100 reactions usually means roughly 4,000-10,000+ impressions.

- Verified posts with 100+ reactions: **1,099** (AI: 550, LinkedIn growth: 307, Data: 242)
- Creators represented: **306**
- `R/1k` = reactions per 1,000 followers. It lets you compare a 20k-follower creator with a 900k one. A hook that beats its author's audience size is the real signal, so read that column before the raw count.

How to use it: don't copy a hook word for word. Take the pattern (the first column of the table below), then fill it with your own true story, company, number or opinion.

## Which hook patterns win (all verified posts)

A post can match several patterns. Sorted by median reactions per 1k followers.

| Pattern | Posts | Median reactions | Median R/1k followers | Best |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Announcement, launch or news | 52 | 598 | 5.7 | 21,100 |
| Question hook | 49 | 636 | 5.5 | 12,268 |
| Humour or relatable feeling | 41 | 693 | 4.6 | 12,946 |
| Free resource or giveaway | 45 | 751 | 4.3 | 5,081 |
| Failure, rejection, layoff or mistake | 39 | 857 | 3.9 | 4,020 |
| Contrarian or myth-busting | 82 | 1,111 | 3.9 | 10,502 |
| Before → after / transformation / milestone | 85 | 852 | 3.9 | 23,549 |
| Money, salary or a hard number | 124 | 724 | 3.7 | 11,407 |
| Named company or famous person | 260 | 807 | 3.6 | 12,946 |
| First-person story or confession | 229 | 772 | 3.3 | 31,313 |
| Explainer or X vs Y | 83 | 657 | 3.2 | 9,290 |
| Career and job hunt | 87 | 743 | 3.2 | 21,100 |
| List, cheat sheet, roadmap or guide | 106 | 739 | 3.1 | 31,313 |
| Plain statement / other | 300 | 689 | 3.1 | 17,683 |
| Interview or hiring-side insight | 65 | 540 | 2.8 | 23,549 |

## Reactions by media type

Only posts whose media type was checked inside the main post (not the related-posts cards under it). This sample is selected for 100+ reactions, so it shows which formats reach high numbers; it does not measure average performance by format.

| Media | Posts | Median reactions | Median R/1k followers |
|---|---|---|---|
| image | 160 | 636 | 3.8 |
| text-only | 75 | 453 | 2.3 |
| video | 23 | 334 | 1.9 |
| document/carousel | 13 | 541 | 3.1 |
| link card | 8 | 481 | 2.0 |
